# API matrix

The endpoint-level run matrices follow this inventory. Each runnable local endpoint has five real scenarios: three different targets in a `hafley-rs` copy, one target in `hafley_scm`, and one in Tokio. Rows include exact calls, return code, wall time, trimmed sample, correctness check, and the closest ryi/ryii comparison. Full raw JSON-RPC, CLI, and HTTP captures are under `~/.cache/lanes/claude-375/eval/bench/runs/api-matrix/`.

| Tool | Documented and executable API inventory | Endpoint evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `ryi` | CLI commands: `fast`, `slow`, `scip`, `capabilities`, `graph`, `cleave`, `move`, `rename`, `query`, `region`, `watch`, `diff`, `ingest`, `schema`, `trail`, `stratify`; exact flags in the per-command rows below. | Per-command matrix follows; help captured from PATH binary. |
| `ryii` | Same 16 CLI commands and flags as `ryi`; binary help captured independently. | Per-command matrix follows; every target was run with both binaries where applicable. |
| `codegraph` | CLI command inventory and flags: see “CodeGraph binary and HTTP surface details”; MCP: 8 named tools with parameter schemas; HTTP UI source registry: 19 method/route entries. | CLI + MCP run matrices include 5 scenarios per endpoint. Mutating/global-config commands, interactive daemons, and unavailable HTTP UI listener are recorded with the reason they were not invoked. |
| `codebase-memory-mcp` | CLI: `cli`, `install`, `uninstall`, `update`, `config list|get|set|reset`, version/help; MCP: 17 tools with complete live parameter schemas; HTTP source registry: 17 routes/methods. | MCP/CLI tool matrix includes 5 scenarios per tool. HTTP rows include source params and transport limitation. |
| `sem` | CLI commands and nested commands/flags are listed from every `--help`; MCP: 13 tools with live `tools/list` schemas; no HTTP routes registered. | CLI and local MCP matrices include 5 scenarios per endpoint. Cloud/auth and mutation actions are separately constrained. |
| `graphify` | Full CLI command/subcommand/flag inventory, including platform install/uninstall endpoints; MCP: 10 tools; resources: 6; HTTP: `POST /mcp`. | CLI, MCP tools/resources, and HTTP matrices include 5 scenarios per endpoint/resource. Dependency/auth failures and retries are recorded. |

All endpoint-level samples are at most 10 output lines. Correctness checks use SCIP target rows or source reads through ryi/ryii. Commands that alter global configuration, install packages, require interactive/authenticated account state, or need the prohibited `/tmp` runtime are marked with their exact constraint rather than invoked.

## ryi and ryii execution matrix
Scenario keys: `copy-fn` = `push_claude_agent` in the hafley-rs bench copy; `copy-type` = `Subscribe` in that copy; `copy-term` = `top` in that copy; `crate-term` = `direct` in the separate `hafley_scm` copy; `other-fn` = `can_auto_advance` in Tokio. Every command used `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=~/.cache/lanes/claude-375/target`; the exact executable argv is shown. Each output sample is one or two lines. `region` used a marker fixture under the benchmark copies and left it unchanged. `diff` used `HEAD` as both revisions because each copied repo has one commit.

### ryi target reads and truth rows

`ryi query` read the selected targets at their source locations: `push_claude_agent` at `crates/boop-harness/src/harness/claude.rs:904`, `CfgNodeKind` at `crates/hafley_scm/src/read/types.rs:1313`, `CONTEXT_VIEW_SCHEMA` at `crates/boop-store/src/ident.rs:4888`, and Tokio `can_auto_advance` at `tokio/src/time/clock.rs:354`. The SCIP truth rows record `push_claude_agent` as fn / 2 refs, `Subscribe` as type / 3 refs, `top` as term / 61 refs, `direct` as term / 2 refs, and `can_auto_advance` as fn / 1 ref. Exact `ryi query` output is in the run log under `~/.cache/lanes/claude-375/eval/bench/runs/api-matrix/`.

`ryi diff` and `ryii diff` on each one-commit copy accepted `--from HEAD --to HEAD` and returned rc 0, with zero changed blobs and 7 resolved-edge removals. That is a mismatch for a same-revision query. `cleave` succeeded on the two hafley-rs symbol scenarios and crate scenario after destination paths were changed to declared module files; the Tokio method target remained unresolved by cleave. `region` returned drift as requested by its read-only default. Those command records include exact stderr and rc.

## Scope and method

- Current date: 2026-09-28. Tool source trees, installed executables, `--help` output, API docs, and prior evaluation reports were read before new runs.
- Target trees: `~/.cache/lanes/claude-375/eval/bench/runs/{codegraph,cbm,sem,graphify,ryii}/{hafley-rs,hafley_scm,<other>}`. The other bench repository selected for this matrix is `tokio`.
- Telemetry environment: `CODEGRAPH_TELEMETRY=0`, `DO_NOT_TRACK=1`, `SEM_TELEMETRY=0`, `GRAPHIFY_TELEMETRY=0`, `CBM_TELEMETRY=0`. No product files are modified. No installs/builds are run. No global config writes. No `/tmp` paths.
- ryi truth checks use existing SCIP indices in `eval/bench/repos/` and/or source reads through `ryi query` and `ryi graph`; code reads use ryi results as requested. `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=~/.cache/lanes/claude-375/target` is set for commands that could invoke Cargo.
- Existing benchmark reports and command captures are supporting evidence only. The matrix rows carry calls, rc, wall time, an output sample, a correctness check, and the paired ryi/ryii result.

## CodeGraph binary and HTTP surface details

Installed executable: `codegraph --help` exposes `init`, `uninit`, `index`, `sync`, `status`, `query`, `explore`, `context`, `node`, `files`, `daemon`/`daemons`, `unlock`, `callers`, `callees`, `impact`, `affected`, `install`, `uninstall`, `telemetry`, `upgrade`, `version`, `help`, and `serve`. Per-command option inventory was captured from `codegraph <command> --help`; details are summarized in the opening API matrix. The installed v1.6.0 binary rejects `ui` as an unknown command. `daemon`/`daemons` is interactive process control and was not started. `install`/`uninstall` and `upgrade` were not run because they edit agent/global configuration or package state.

### CodeGraph CLI command and flag inventory

| Endpoint | Flags from executable help |
|---|---|
| `init` | `-i/--index` (deprecated), `-f/--force`, `-v/--verbose`, `-y/--yes` |
| `uninit` | `-f/--force` |
| `index` | `-f/--force`, `-q/--quiet`, `-v/--verbose` |
| `sync` | `-q/--quiet` |
| `status` | `-j/--json` |
| `query` | `-p/--path`, `-l/--limit`, `-k/--kind`, `-j/--json` |
| `explore` | `-p/--path`, `--max-files` |
| `context` | `-p/--path`, `-f/--format`, `-n/--max-nodes`, `--no-code` |
| `node` | `-p/--path`, `-f/--file`, `--offset`, `--limit`, `--symbols-only` |
| `files` | `-p/--path`, `--filter`, `--pattern`, `--format`, `--max-depth`, `--no-metadata`, `-j/--json` |
| `daemon` | no flags in executable help |
| `daemons` | no flags in executable help |
| `unlock` | no flags in executable help |
| `version` | no flags in executable help |
| `help` | no flags in executable help |
| `callers` | `-p/--path`, `-l/--limit`, `-j/--json` |
| `callees` | `-p/--path`, `-l/--limit`, `-j/--json` |
| `impact` | `-p/--path`, `-d/--depth`, `-j/--json` |
| `affected` | `-p/--path`, `--stdin`, `-d/--depth`, `-f/--filter`, `-j/--json`, `-q/--quiet` |
| `install` | `-t/--target`, `-l/--location`, `-y/--yes`, `-i/--init`, `--no-permissions`, `--print-config`, `--refresh` |
| `uninstall` | `-t/--target`, `-l/--location`, `-y/--yes`, `--keep-cli` |
| `telemetry` | positional `status|on|off`; no options |
| `upgrade` | optional version, `--check`, `-f/--force` |
| `serve` | `-p/--path`, `--mcp`, `--no-watch` |

### CodeGraph MCP tools and parameter schemas

The schemas below are from the installed server registry. `projectPath` is optional on every tool; the server was started with `--path` set to each benchmark copy.

| MCP endpoint | Required and optional parameters |

| `codegraph_callers` | `symbol` required string; `file` optional string; `limit` number default 20; `projectPath` optional string |
| `codegraph_callees` | `symbol` required string; `file` optional string; `limit` number default 20; `projectPath` optional string |
| `codegraph_impact` | `symbol` required string; `file` optional string; `depth` number default 2; `projectPath` optional string |
| `codegraph_node` | `symbol` optional string; `includeCode` boolean default false; `file` optional string; `offset`, `limit`, `line` optional numbers; `symbolsOnly` boolean default false; `projectPath` optional string |
| `codegraph_explore` | `query` required string; `maxFiles` number default 12; `projectPath` optional string |
| `codegraph_status` | `projectPath` optional string |
| `codegraph_files` | `path`, `pattern` optional strings; `format` enum `tree,flat,grouped` default `tree`; `includeMetadata` boolean default true; `maxDepth` optional number; `projectPath` optional string |

## Codebase-memory-mcp complete API matrix

The installed `codebase-memory-mcp --help` surface is `cli`, `install`, `uninstall`, `update`, `config list|get|set|reset`, `--version`, and `--help`. `cli` takes `--quiet`, `--progress`, `--verbose`, or `--json`, then a tool name and one JSON argument object. Per-tool parameters below come from the live MCP `tools/list` registry and therefore cover the JSON CLI dispatcher as well as stdio MCP. The registry and exact JSON-RPC requests are captured in `~/.cache/lanes/claude-375/eval/bench/runs/api-matrix/cbm/`. Install/config-writing commands were excluded by the no-global-config-edits constraint.

### Codebase-memory-mcp CLI endpoint and option inventory

| Endpoint | Executable syntax and flags | Run status |
|---|---|---|
| `cli` dispatcher | `cli [--quiet] [--progress] [--verbose] [--json] <tool_name> [json_args]`; `json_args` is one JSON object. Each MCP tool row below was also called through the CLI dispatcher. | Tool calls are in the endpoint matrix. |
| `install` | `install [-y|-n] [--force] [--dry-run] [--dir=<path>] [--skip-config] [--clients=<tokens>]` | Not run: may write global/client configuration. |
| `uninstall` | `uninstall [-y|-n] [--dry-run]` | Not run: may remove global/client configuration. |
| `update` | `update [-y|-n]` | Not run: modifies installed package state. |
| `config list` | `config list` | Read-only endpoint not separately invoked; no configuration changes were made. |
| `config get` | `config get <key>` | Read-only endpoint not separately invoked; no configuration changes were made. |
| `config set` | `config set <key> <value>` | Not run: global config write. |
| `config reset` | `config reset` | Not run: global config write. |
| `--version`, `--help` | top-level flags | Help/version captured. |
| UI options | `--ui=true|false`, `--port=<N>`, `--tool-profile=analysis|scout` | HTTP listener not started due prohibited system-temp runtime; route inventory follows. |

### Tool run matrix

Each row names one endpoint. Each entry includes the exact MCP `tools/call` JSON request (equivalent args go to `codebase-memory-mcp cli --json <tool> <json>`), scenario, effective rc (`0` for a non-error tool result, `1` when `isError=true`), wall time, and a real response sample. Three `hafley-rs` contexts use distinct targets/files; `hafley_scm` and Tokio are additional repository contexts. Source truth came from targeted `ryi query` captures; the closest command/result is repeated by scenario. Correctness is scored against the answer the endpoint is meant to return, not just process exit.

The run suite used stdio and the isolated CBM cache. HTTP listener calls were not made: server startup depends on its coordinated local daemon/runtime endpoint, whose documented default is under the system temp directory; this task prohibits `/tmp` use. Read/write route effects are included in the API inventory. Static route rows below are source-registered; no product build or install was performed.
| HTTP method / route | Documented request parameters or body | Execution status |
|---|---|---|
| `OPTIONS /rpc and UI routes` | CORS preflight; no params | Not invoked; HTTP listener unavailable under no-/tmp constraint. |
| `GET /__cbm/ui-readiness` | private daemon challenge proof | Not invoked; source-only. |
| `POST /rpc` | JSON-RPC MCP request envelope | MCP equivalent called over stdio; HTTP transport itself not invoked. |
| `GET /api/layout*` | project/query options parsed by layout handler | Not invoked; source-only. |
| `GET /api/repo-info*` | project query | Not invoked; source-only. |
| `POST /api/index` | JSON `{root_path, project_name}`; starts indexing | Not invoked; same index API was exercised over MCP on benchmark copies. |
| `GET /api/index-status` | none | Not invoked; source-only. |
| `GET /api/ui-config` | none | Not invoked; source-only. |
| `DELETE /api/project*` | `name` query; deletes project DB | Not invoked; MCP `delete_project` exercised only on disposable copies. |
| `GET /api/browse*` | `path` query | Not invoked; source-only. |
| `GET /api/adr*` | `project` query | Not invoked; read-only MCP `manage_adr(mode=outline)` exercised. |
| `POST /api/adr` | JSON `{project, content}`; writes ADR | Not invoked; source-only, write route. |
| `GET /api/project-health*` | `name` query | Not invoked; source-only. |
| `GET /api/processes` | none | Not invoked; source-only. |
| `GET /api/logs*` | `lines` query | Not invoked; source-only. |
| `GET /` | embedded `index.html` | Not invoked; source-only. |
| `GET /assets/...` | embedded asset path | Not invoked; source-only. |

### Retry record

Initial CBM requests for `trace_path` supplied short names; after rereading the schema/docs, retries used the exact qualified names from `search_graph` and returned successfully. `get_code_snippet` retries likewise used returned qualified names. The initial Tokio change query named branch `main`; the copied Tokio Git tree uses `master`, and retry with `base_branch=master` returned successfully. No unresolved tool errors remain in the retried cases. Four disposable project indexes were created and deleted with `index_repository`/`delete_project`; no source copy was altered.

### Live MCP schemas

The live stdio `tools/list` registry returned the following 13 tools. JSON fields, required status, types, and descriptions are captured from `tools-list.json`; local tools were called across the five repository scenarios. The four review tools require a real sem-cloud diff/branch ID. They were limited to the earlier empty-argument validation calls because no review ID was supplied and `reply_to_branch` can send an external message.

| MCP tool | Parameter schema |
|---|---|
| `join_review` | `diff_id`:string required (The sem-cloud diff id to listen on (from the review URL).) |
| `list_open_branches` | `diff_id`:string required (The diff id to inspect (same one passed to join_review).) |
| `reply_to_branch` | `comment_id`:string required (The commentId of the branch/question being answered (from the branch payload wait_for_branch returned).); `content`:string required (The answer text. When partial is true this must be the FULL cumulative answer composed so far, not just the newest chunk — each partial call replaces the streamed text.); `diff_id`:string required (The diff id the comment belongs to.); `partial`:['boolean', 'null'] (true while still composing (streams `content` so the reviewer watches it build); omit or pass false on the final call, which commits the answer.) |
| `sem_blame` | `file_path`:string required (Path to the file (relative to repo root or absolute)) |
| `sem_callers` | `file`:['string', 'null'] (Restrict to the definition in this file (disambiguates).); `format`:['string', 'null'] (Output format: "text" (default) or "json".); `limit`:['integer', 'null'] (Return at most this many callers (all by default).); `query`:string required (Entity whose callers to list, optionally as "type name" (e.g. "function createProgram") to disambiguate by kind. Must resolve to exactly one definition; an ambiguous name is refused with the full candidate list.) |
| `sem_context` | `entities`:['array', 'null'] (Several target entities to pack in one call (batch form of `entity_name`), each packed under the same token_budget. Text output concatenates the per-entity blocks; json output is newline-delimited, one object per entity.); `entity_name`:['string', 'null'] (Name of the target entity); `file_path`:['string', 'null'] (Path to the file containing the entity. OPTIONAL: omit it for one-call lookup — the entity_name is resolved across the whole repo (ambiguity returns a compact candidate list).); `format`:['string', 'null'] (Output format: "text" (default) or "json".); `fresh`:['boolean', 'null'] (Force a full re-send even if this session already received an identical fill for the entity (use when your context was compacted and the earlier body is gone).); `hops`:['integer', 'null'] (Bound related entities to this many graph hops from the target (0 or omitted = unbounded, fill to the token budget). Use e.g. 1-2 for the immediate neighborhood.); `mode`:['string', 'null'] (Set to "headers" to render each packed entity as its header (signature plus first doc-comment line) instead of its body — the same token_budget buys a much wider map.); `no_default_excludes`:['boolean', 'null'] (Include files and directories excluded by default, including generated, fixture, vendor, and benchmark paths.); `token_budget`:['integer', 'null'] (Maximum token budget. Defaults to 8000.) |
| `sem_diff` | `base_ref`:['string', 'null'] (Base ref to compare from (branch, tag, or commit hash, e.g. 'main'). If omitted, shows working-tree changes (like `sem diff`).); `file_path`:['string', 'null'] (Optional: diff only this file); `target_ref`:['string', 'null'] (Target ref to compare to. Defaults to HEAD.) |
| `sem_entities` | `format`:['string', 'null'] (Output format: "text" (default) or "json".); `limit`:['integer', 'null'] (Max results for `query` mode (default 10).); `no_default_excludes`:['boolean', 'null'] (Include files and directories excluded by default, including generated, fixture, vendor, and benchmark paths.); `path`:['string', 'null'] (Optional path to a file or directory. If omitted, defaults to '.'.); `query`:['string', 'null'] (Optional free-text query to find entities by intent across the whole repo (e.g. "where is the retry logic"), when you don't know the entity name. Ranks by name/signature relevance and graph centrality; returns file:line and dependent counts. Ignores `path` when set.); `signatures`:['boolean', 'null'] (Show each listed entity's header: its signature up to the body plus the first doc-comment line — more than a name, far less than a body.); `text`:['string', 'null'] (Exact substring to search for inside entity bodies across the whole repo (use instead of grep for strings, error messages, config keys). Case-sensitive. Hits come back entity-addressed: file, innermost entity, line, matched line text.) |
| `sem_find` | `file`:['string', 'null'] (Restrict to entities defined in this file.); `format`:['string', 'null'] (Output format: "text" (default) or "json".); `queries`:['array', 'null'] (Several names to look up in one call (batch form of `query`). Each resolves independently; a miss on one never affects the others.); `query`:['string', 'null'] (Entity name to look up, optionally as "type name" (e.g. "function createProgram") to disambiguate by kind.) |
| `sem_grep` | `case_insensitive`:['boolean', 'null'] (Case-insensitive match.); `format`:['string', 'null'] (Output format: "text" (default) or "json".); `pattern`:['string', 'null'] (Regex or literal pattern to search file contents for (rg-compatible).); `patterns`:['array', 'null'] (Several patterns to search in one call (batch form of `pattern`). Each pattern's hits are reported separately, never merged.) |
| `sem_impact` | `entity_name`:string required (Name of the entity to analyze impact for); `file_path`:string required (Path to the file containing the entity); `mode`:['string', 'null'] (Analysis mode: 'all' (default, shows deps + dependents + transitive impact + tests), 'deps' (direct dependencies only), 'dependents' (direct dependents only), 'tests' (affected test entities only)); `no_default_excludes`:['boolean', 'null'] (Include files and directories excluded by default, including generated, fixture, vendor, and benchmark paths.) |
| `sem_log` | `entity_name`:['string', 'null'] (Name of the entity to trace history for. Omit for repo-level history analytics: hotspots (most-changed entities) and co-change pairs (entities that change in the same commits).); `file_path`:['string', 'null'] (Path to the file containing the entity. If omitted, auto-detects.); `limit`:['integer', 'null'] (Maximum number of commits to analyze. Defaults to 50.) |
| `wait_for_branch` | `diff_id`:string required (The diff id to poll (same one passed to join_review).); `wait_seconds`:['integer', 'null'] (How long to long-poll for the next reviewer question, in seconds (default 40, clamped to 45). Keep calling this tool back-to-back — on timeout or right after replying — for as long as you are listening.) |

### HTTP surface

No HTTP route or HTTP server is documented or registered in this sem build. MCP transport is newline-delimited stdio JSON-RPC.


## Graphify complete API matrix

### Installed CLI inventory from docs and binary

Installed `graphify --help` is saved at `~/.cache/lanes/claude-375/eval/bench/runs/api-matrix/graphify/binary-help.txt`. README examples add `prs --triage`, `--conflicts`, `--worktrees`, `--base`, and `--repo`; these appear below with the binary options. Each row names one command endpoint.

| CLI endpoint | Documented and binary usage/flags |
|---|---|
| `install` | `install [--platform P]` |
| `uninstall` | `uninstall [--purge]` |
| `path` | `path "A" "B" [--graph PATH]` |
| `explain` | `explain "X" [--graph PATH]` |
| `diagnose multigraph` | `diagnose multigraph [--graph PATH] [--json] [--max-examples N] [--directed|--undirected] [--extract-path PATH]` |
| `clone` | `clone <github-url> [--branch BRANCH] [--out DIR]` |
| `merge-driver` | `merge-driver <base> <current> <other>` |
| `merge-graphs` | `merge-graphs <g1> <g2> [--out PATH]` |
| `add` | `add <url> [--author NAME] [--contributor NAME] [--dir PATH]` |
| `watch` | `watch <path>` |
| `update` | `update <path> [--force] [--no-cluster]` |

### MCP schemas from the live registry

The installed virtual environment exposes MCP SDK and the server initialized as Graphify 0.9.70. All ten tools accept optional `project_path` (absolute project directory containing `graphify-out/graph.json`). The live registry is saved in `tools-list.json`; resource names are saved in `resources-list.json`.

| MCP tool | Parameters |
|---|---|
| `query_graph` | `question`:string required; `mode`:string enum=bfs,dfs default='bfs'; `depth`:integer default=3; `token_budget`:integer default=2000; `context_filter`:array; `project_path`:string |
| `get_node` | `label`:string; `node_id`:string; `project_path`:string |
| `get_neighbors` | `label`:string; `node_id`:string; `relation_filter`:string; `token_budget`:integer default=2000; `project_path`:string |
| `get_community` | `community_id`:integer required; `token_budget`:integer default=2000; `project_path`:string |
| `god_nodes` | `top_n`:integer default=10; `exclude_hubs_percentile`:number; `project_path`:string |
| `graph_stats` | `project_path`:string |
| `shortest_path` | `source`:string required; `target`:string required; `max_hops`:integer default=8; `undirected`:boolean default=False; `project_path`:string |
| `list_prs` | `base`:string; `repo`:string; `project_path`:string |
| `get_pr_impact` | `pr_number`:integer required; `repo`:string; `project_path`:string |
| `triage_prs` | `base`:string; `repo`:string; `project_path`:string |

### Corrected retries and unavailable operations

| Endpoint | Request/result | Constraint or retry result |
|---|---|---|
| `install, uninstall, all platform install/uninstall, hook install/uninstall` | Skipped: these edit or remove platform/config files; no global config edits. | Captured in scenario logs. |
| `clone, add` | Skipped: remote fetch and corpus writes. | Captured in scenario logs. |
| `label` | Skipped: requires an LLM provider; no provider credentials were supplied. | Captured in scenario logs. |
| `provider add/remove, global add/remove` | Skipped: these mutate provider/global graph configuration. | Captured in scenario logs. |
| `provider show openai` | Called on all five contexts; output `Provider 'openai' not found.` `provider list` returned `No custom providers registered.`, so no configured provider name was available. | Captured in scenario logs. |
| `watch` | Called on five contexts with documented `watch <path>`; all returned `error: watchdog not installed. Run: pip install watchdog`. No install was run. | Captured in scenario logs. |
| `export svg` | Called on five graph files with documented `--graph`; all returned `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'matplotlib'`. No install was run. | Captured in scenario logs. |
| `prs / list_prs` | Read-only calls returned `Error: gh CLI not found or not authenticated. Run: gh auth login`. No login was performed. | Captured in scenario logs. |
| `triage_prs` | Five calls returned the same GitHub CLI/auth error before ranking; provider credentials were removed. | Captured in scenario logs. |
| `POST /mcp initialize` | HTTP 200; initialization response reports Graphify 0.9.70. | Captured in scenario logs. |

Graphify `path`/`explain` initial labels were retried using exact node IDs. All five `explain` retries succeeded; four `path` retries found directed routes, and the `CfgNodeKind` pair returned `No directed path found` with rc 0. Two update requests over the first 12-second harness limit were retried with 60 seconds and completed. `get_pr_impact` on Tokio was retried with `repo=tokio-rs/tokio`, returning PR #1 details. The `ryi query` call on `push_claude_agent` returned `connection closed before message completed`; paired `ryii query` succeeded at `claude.rs:904`.
